std.critical_section(
    string - optional, create a named critical section, if no name specified, creates a code-local critical section.
For explanation: named critical section can be across different code sections, while non-named critical sections are bound to the scope in which they are in.
) -> std.critical_section

    +-------------------------+

    +-------------------------------------------+
    | Example: combined_str = print(var1, var2, var3); |
    +-------------------------------------------+

        --------------------------------------------------
std.random_hash()

  - Get current date and time in DD-MM-YYYY HH:MM:SS format
        --------------------------------------------------
std.resolve_source_file(
    path: string - Path to the script file,
    remove_comments: int/bool - Optional. 1 removes comments from the resulting text,
    remove_strings: int/bool - Optional. 1 masks string literals in the resulting text
)

  - Resolve a .shio file recursively and return full source with includes expanded
        --------------------------------------------------
std.resolve_source_text(
    source: string - Source code text to resolve,
    source_dir: string - Base directory used for resolving include paths,
    remove_comments: int/bool - Optional. 1 removes comments from the resulting text,
    remove_strings: int/bool - Optional. 1 masks string literals in the resulting text
)

  - Resolve source text recursively using a base directory and return full source with includes expanded
        --------------------------------------------------
std.runtime_error()

  - Print to console output
        --------------------------------------------------
std.save_config(
    package: string - Config package/folder name (safe token),
    key: string - Config key/file name without extension (safe token),
    json: json - JSON value to serialize and write to disk
)

  - Save JSON config to user config path (Windows: %USERPROFILE%/Documents/Shizoscript/Configs, Linux: $XDG_CONFIG_HOME/shizoscript/configs or ~/.config/shizoscript/configs)
        --------------------------------------------------
std.second()

  - Get current second as number (0-60)
        --------------------------------------------------
std.sleep(
    milliseconds: Duration to pause execution
)

  - Suspend script execution for a duration (async)
        --------------------------------------------------
std.string(
    value: string - Initial string values to concatenate
) -> std.string

  - Create a new string, optionally initialized with given values that are concatenated
        --------------------------------------------------
std.system(
    command: string - Command string to be passed to the system shell,
    capture: int/bool - Optional. 1 to capture stdout+stderr and return it as string,
    print_output: int/bool - Optional. Only used when capture=1. 1 prints captured output to console, 0 suppresses it
)

  - Execute a shell command. If capture is enabled, returns the command output (stdout+stderr). Otherwise returns the exit code.
        --------------------------------------------------
std.system_path(
    path: string - Path with environment variables
)

  - Expand environment variables and normalize a filesystem path
        --------------------------------------------------
std.thread(

    callback: function - The function to be executed in the new thread
) -> std.thread

  - threading
        --------------------------------------------------
std.timestamp()

  - Get current date and time in DD-MM-YYYY HH:MM:SS format
        --------------------------------------------------

)

  - Get the user config path (Windows: %USERPROFILE%/Documents/Shizoscript/Configs, Linux: $XDG_CONFIG_HOME/shizoscript/configs or ~/.config/shizoscript/configs)

==================================================
Object: audio.audio_fft
==================================================

        --------------------------------------------------
attack()

  - Sets attack speed (0.001 - 1.0). Higher = faster rise.
        --------------------------------------------------
band_ema()

  - Returns the exponential moving average values for each band
        --------------------------------------------------
band_ema_value()

  - Returns the EMA value for a specific band index
        --------------------------------------------------
band_normalized()

  - Returns the normalized value for a specific band index
        --------------------------------------------------
band_smoothing()

  - Sets the spatial smoothing radius (0-50). Blends each band with that many neighbors. Higher = smoother output.
        --------------------------------------------------
decay()

  - Sets adaptive peak decay (0.8 - 0.9999). Lower = peaks reset faster (more dynamic).
        --------------------------------------------------
high_cutoff()

  - Mid/high crossover in Hz
        --------------------------------------------------
high_energy()

  - High-frequency energy (auto noise-floor, 0..1)
        --------------------------------------------------
last_time()

  - Returns seconds since last FFT calculation
        --------------------------------------------------
low_cutoff()

  - Low/mid crossover in Hz
        --------------------------------------------------
low_energy()

  - Low-frequency energy (auto noise-floor, 0..1)
        --------------------------------------------------
magnitudes()

  - Returns the current FFT magnitude values
        --------------------------------------------------
mid_energy()

  - Mid-frequency energy (auto noise-floor, 0..1)
        --------------------------------------------------
noise_floor()

  - Sets noise-floor threshold (0.0 - 0.9). Values below this are suppressed to 0.
        --------------------------------------------------
normalized_bands()

  - Returns the normalized band values (processed audio data)
        --------------------------------------------------
release()

  - Sets release speed (0.001 - 1.0). Higher = faster decay.
        --------------------------------------------------
sample_rate()

  - Returns the current sample rate
        --------------------------------------------------

==================================================
Object: webserver.http_server
==================================================

    port    -> variable
    running -> variable

        --------------------------------------------------
hash_password(
    password: string - plaintext password,
    iterations: int (optional) - PBKDF2 iterations, default 100000,
    salt_len: int (optional) - salt length in bytes, default 16
)

  - Hash a password using PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256
        --------------------------------------------------
init(
    port: int - Server port number
)

  - Initialize HTTP server with port
        --------------------------------------------------
route(
    method: string - HTTP method (GET, POST, PUT, DELETE, *),
    path: string - URL path pattern (regex),
    callback: function(request_object, writer) - Handler function with two parameters:
  1. request_object: JSON table with keys: method, path, query, body, client_ip, client_port, is_secure (0/1), timestamp, host, user_agent, content_type, content_length, headers (json), cookies (json), query_params (json), matches (json), multipart (json) with is_multipart, parsed, error, fields, parts
  2. writer: shz_stream_writer - Stream writer for chunked/streaming responses. Use write_header(), write(), and end() to send data incrementally. If the writer is used, do not return a result table - the function returns void.
)

  - Register a route handler for HTTP requests
        --------------------------------------------------
start()

  - Start the HTTP server
        --------------------------------------------------
static(
    url_pattern: string - URL pattern (e.g. '/static' or '/files'),
    file_or_directory: string - Path to file or directory to serve (will be protected against directory traversal),
    protect_callback: function(request_object) -> bool - Optional protection callback. The request_object is a JSON table with the following keys:
  method: string - HTTP method
  path: string - Request URL path
  query: string - Raw query string
  body: string - Request body
  client_ip: string - Client IP address
  client_port: int - Client port
  is_secure: int - Whether connection is secure (0/1)
  timestamp: int - Unix timestamp in milliseconds
  host: string - Host header value
  user_agent: string - User-Agent header
  content_type: string - Content-Type header
  content_length: int - Content-Length value
  headers: json - All request headers
  cookies: json - Parsed cookies
  query_params: json - Parsed query parameters
  multipart: json - Multipart form data info with is_multipart, parsed, error, fields, parts
  The callback must return true (shzvar true) to allow serving the file, or false/nil to deny access (returns 404).
)

  - Register a static file route
        --------------------------------------------------
stop()

  - Stop the HTTP server
        --------------------------------------------------
verify_password(
    password: string - plaintext password,
    stored_hash: string - stored hash produced by hash_password
)

  - Verify a password against a PBKDF2 hash
        --------------------------------------------------


==================================================
Object: webserver.https_server
==================================================

    port    -> variable
    running -> variable

        --------------------------------------------------
hash_password(
    password: string - plaintext password,
    iterations: int (optional) - PBKDF2 iterations, default 100000,
    salt_len: int (optional) - salt length in bytes, default 16
)

  - Hash a password using PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256
        --------------------------------------------------
init(
    port: int - Server port number,
    cert_file: string - Path to certificate file,
    key_file: string - Path to private key file
)

  - Initialize HTTPS server with port and certificate files
        --------------------------------------------------
route(
    method: string - HTTP method (GET, POST, PUT, DELETE, *),
    path: string - URL path pattern (regex),
    callback: function(request_object, writer) - Handler function with two parameters:
  1. request_object: JSON table with keys: method, path, query, body, client_ip, client_port, is_secure (always 1 for HTTPS), timestamp, host, user_agent, content_type, content_length, headers (json), cookies (json), query_params (json), matches (json), multipart (json) with is_multipart, parsed, error, fields, parts
  2. writer: shz_stream_writer - Stream writer for chunked/streaming responses. Use write_header(), write(), and end() to send data incrementally. If the writer is used, do not return a result table - the function returns void.
)

  - Register a route handler for HTTPS requests
        --------------------------------------------------
start()

  - Start the HTTPS server
        --------------------------------------------------
static(
    url_pattern: string - URL pattern (e.g. '/static' or '/files'),
    file_or_directory: string - Path to file or directory to serve (will be protected against directory traversal),
    protect_callback: function(request_object) -> bool - Optional protection callback. The request_object is a JSON table with the following keys:
  method: string - HTTP method
  path: string - Request URL path
  query: string - Raw query string
  body: string - Request body
  client_ip: string - Client IP address
  client_port: int - Client port
  is_secure: int - Whether connection is secure (always 1 for HTTPS)
  timestamp: int - Unix timestamp in milliseconds
  host: string - Host header value
  user_agent: string - User-Agent header
  content_type: string - Content-Type header
  content_length: int - Content-Length value
  headers: json - All request headers
  cookies: json - Parsed cookies
  query_params: json - Parsed query parameters
  multipart: json - Multipart form data info with is_multipart, parsed, error, fields, parts
  The callback must return true (shzvar true) to allow serving the file, or false/nil to deny access (returns 404).
)

  - Register a static file route
        --------------------------------------------------
stop()

  - Stop the HTTPS server
        --------------------------------------------------
verify_password(
    password: string - plaintext password,
    stored_hash: string - stored hash produced by hash_password
)

  - Verify a password against a PBKDF2 hash
        --------------------------------------------------
